class="interlanguage-link-target"><span>Polski</span></a></li><li class="interlanguage-link interwiki-pnb mw-list-item"><a data-language-autonym="Português" data-language-local-name="Portuguese" class="interlanguage-link-target"><span>Português</span></a></li><li class="interlanguage-link interwiki-ro mw-list-item"><a English</span></a></li><li class="interlanguage-link interwiki-sk mw-list-item"><a href="https://sk.wikipedia.org/wiki/Te%C3%B3ria_inform%C3%A1cie" title="Teória informácie – Slovak" lang="sk" hreflang="sk" data-title="Teória informácie" data-language-autonym="Slovenčina" data-language-local-name="Slovak" class="interlanguage-link-target"><span>Slovenčina</span></a></li><li class="interlanguage-link interwiki-sl mw-list-item"><a href="https://sl.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teorija_informacij" title="Teorija informacij – Slovenian" lang="sl" hreflang="sl" data-title="Teorija data-language-autonym="Shqip" data-language-local-name="Albanian" class="interlanguage-link-target"><span>Shqip</span></a></li><li class="interlanguage-link interwiki-sr mw-list-item"><a href="https://sr.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%A2%D0%B5%D0%BE%D1%80%D0%B8%D1%98%D0%B0_%D0%B8%D0%BD%D1%84%D0%BE%D1%80%D0%BC%D0%B0%D1%86%D0%B8%D1%98a" title="Теорија информацијa – Serbian" lang="sr" hreflang="sr" data-title="Теорија информацијa" data-language-autonym="Српски / srpski" data-language-local-name="Serbian" class="interlanguage-link-target"><span>Српски / srpski</span></a></li><li class="interlanguage-link interwiki-sv mw-list-item"><a href="https://sv.wikipedia.org/wiki/Informationsteori" title="Informationsteori – Swedish" lang="sv" hreflang="sv" data-title="Informationsteori" data-language-autonym="Svenska" data-language-local-name="Swedish" class="interlanguage-link-target"><span>Svenska</span></a></li><li class="interlanguage-link interwiki-ta mw-list-item"><a href="https://ta.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E0%AE%A4%E0%AE%95%E0%AE%B5%E0%AE%B2%E0%AF%8D_%E0%AE%95%E0%AF%8B%E0%AE%9F%E0%AF%8D%E0%AE%AA%E0%AE%BE%E0%AE%9F%E0%AF%81" title="தகவல் கோட்ப"mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-LXteS_318-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"LXteS"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-LXteS-318"}}'><a href="#cite_note-LXteS-318" id="mwCXk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCXo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCXs">[</span>315<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCXw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwCX0">Critics have accused kernel developers of covering up security flaws, or at least not announcing them; in 2008, Torvalds responded to this with the following:<sup about="#mwt1243" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-YS7OI_319-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"YS7OI"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-YS7OI-319"}}'><a href="#cite_note-YS7OI-319" id="mwCX4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCX8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCYA">[</span>316<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCYE">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1246" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Ps5dB_320-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Ps5dB"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Ps5dB-320"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Ps5dB-320" id="mwCYI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCYM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCYQ">[</span>317<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCYU">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1244412712" about="#mwt1247" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwCYY" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"blockquote","href":"./Template:Blockquote"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"I personally consider security bugs to be just \"normal bugs\". I don&apos;t cover them up, but I also don&apos;t have any reason what-so-ever to think it&apos;s a good idea to track them and announce them as something special...one reason I refuse to bother with the whole security circus is that I think it glorifies—and thus encourages—the wrong behavior. It makes \"heroes\" out of security people, as if the people who don&apos;t just fix normal bugs aren&apos;t as important. In fact, all the boring normal bugs are &apos;&apos;way&apos;&apos; more important, just because there&apos;s[sic] a lot more of them. I don&apos;t think some spectacular security hole should be glorified or cared about as being any more \"special\" than a random spectacular crash due to bad locking."}},"i":0}}]}'/><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt1247" id="mwCYc"><p>I personally consider security bugs to be just "normal bugs". I don't cover them up, but I also don't have any reason what-so-ever to think it's a good idea to track them and announce them as something special...one reason I refuse to bother with the whole security circus is that I think it glorifies—and thus encourages—the wrong behavior. It makes "heroes" out of security people, as if the people who don't just fix normal bugs aren't as important. In fact, all the boring normal bugs are <i>way</i> more important, just because there's[sic] a lot more of them. I don't think some spectacular security hole should be glorified or cared about as being any more "special" than a random spectacular crash due to bad locking.</p></blockquote>

<p id="mwCYg">Linux distributions typically release security updates to fix vulnerabilities in the Linux kernel. Many offer <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Long-term_support" title="Long-term support" id="mwCYk">long-term support</a> releases that receive security updates for a certain Linux kernel version for an extended period of time.</p>

<p id="mwCYo">In 2024, researchers disclosed that the Linux kernel contained a serious vulnerability, CVE-2024-50264, located in the AF_VSOCK subsystem. This bug is a use-after-free flaw, a class of memory corruption issue that occurs when a program continues to use memory after it has been freed.<sup about="#mwt1251" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-321" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-321"}}'><a href="#cite_note-321" id="mwCYs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCYw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCY0">[</span>318<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCY4">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1254" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-322" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-322"}}'><a href="#cite_note-322" id="mwCY8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCZA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCZE">[</span>319<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCZI">]</span></span></a></sup>  Such flaws are particularly dangerous in the kernel, as they can allow attackers to escalate privileges. The bug was resolved in May 2025.<sup about="#mwt1257" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-323" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-323"}}'><a href="#cite_note-323" id="mwCZM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCZQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCZU">[</span>320<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCZY">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="38" id="mwCZc" aria-labelledby="Legal"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Legal">Legal</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Linux_kernel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=38" title="Edit section: Legal"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<section data-mw-section-id="39" id="mwCZg" aria-labelledby="Licensing_terms"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Licensing_terms">Licensing terms</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Linux_kernel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=39" title="Edit section: Licensing terms"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwCZk">Initially, Torvalds released Linux under a license that forbade any commercial use.<sup about="#mwt1260" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-hiroo_324-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"hiroo"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-hiroo-324"}}'><a href="#cite_note-hiroo-324" id="mwCZo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCZs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCZw">[</span>321<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCZ0">]</span></span></a></sup> This was changed in version 0.12 by a switch to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GNU_General_Public_License" title="GNU General Public License" id="mwCZ4">GNU General Public License</a> version 2 (GPLv2).<sup about="#mwt1261" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Relnotes-0.12_26-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Relnotes-0.12"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Relnotes-0.12-26" id="mwCZ8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCaA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCaE">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCaI">]</span></span></a></sup> This license allows distribution and sale of possibly modified and unmodified versions of Linux but requires that all those copies be released under the same license and be accompanied by - or that, on request, free access is given to - the complete corresponding source code.<sup about="#mwt1264" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ts0p8_325-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ts0p8"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-ts0p8-325"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ts0p8-325" id="mwCaM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCaQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCaU">[</span>322<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCaY">]</span></span></a></sup> Torvalds has described licensing Linux under the GPLv2 as the "best thing I ever did".<sup about="#mwt1265" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-hiroo_324-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"hiroo"}}'><a href="#cite_note-hiroo-324" id="mwCac"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCag"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCak">[</span>321<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCao">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwCas">The Linux kernel is licensed explicitly under <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GNU_General_Public_License" title="GNU General Public License" id="mwCaw">GNU General Public License</a> version 2 only (GPL-2.0-only) with an explicit syscall exception (Linux-syscall-note),<sup about="#mwt1266" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-COPYING_File_11-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"COPYING_File"}}'><a href="#cite_note-COPYING_File-11" id="mwCa0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCa4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCa8">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCbA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1267" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-kernel.org_GPL-2.0_14-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"kernel.org GPL-2.0"}}'><a href="#cite_note-kernel.org_GPL-2.0-14" id="mwCbE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCbI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCbM">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCbQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1268" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-kernel.org_Linux-syscall-note_15-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"kernel.org Linux-syscall-note"}}'><a href="#cite_note-kernel.org_Linux-syscall-note-15" id="mwCbU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCbY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCbc">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCbg">]</span></span></a></sup> without offering the licensee the option to choose any later version, which is a common GPL extension. Contributed code must be available under <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GPL_compatibility" title="GPL compatibility" class="mw-redirect" id="mwCbk">GPL-compatible license</a>.<sup about="#mwt1269" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Linux_Licensing_16-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Linux_Licensing"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Linux_Licensing-16" id="mwCbo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCbs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCbw">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCb0">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1270" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:9_213-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":9"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:9-213" id="mwCb4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCb8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCcA">[</span>210<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCcE">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwCcI">There was considerable debate about how easily the license could be changed to use later GPL versions (including version 3), and whether this change is even desirable.<sup about="#mwt1273" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BahZO_326-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BahZO"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-BahZO-326"}}'><a href="#cite_note-BahZO-326" id="mwCcM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCcQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCcU">[</span>323<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCcY">]</span></span></a></sup> Torvalds himself specifically indicated upon the release of version 2.4.0 that his own code is released only under version 2.<sup about="#mwt1276" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-9g8Ma_327-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"9g8Ma"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-9g8Ma-327"}}'><a href="#cite_note-9g8Ma-327" id="mwCcc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCcg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCck">[</span>324<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCco">]</span></span></a></sup> The terms of the GPL state that if no version is specified, then any version may be used,<sup about="#mwt1279" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-v5UoJ_328-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"v5UoJ"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-v5UoJ-328"}}'><a href="#cite_note-v5UoJ-328" id="mwCcs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCcw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCc0">[</span>325<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCc4">]</span></span></a></sup> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alan_Cox_(computer_programmer)" title="Alan Cox (computer programmer)" id="mwCc8">Alan Cox</a> pointed out that very few other Linux contributors had specified a particular version of the GPL.<sup about="#mwt1282" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-au8PE_329-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"au8PE"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-au8PE-329"}}'><a href="#cite_note-au8PE-329" id="mwCdA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCdE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCdI">[</span>326<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCdM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwCdQ">In September 2006, a survey of 29 key kernel programmers indicated that 28 preferred GPLv2 to the then-current GPLv3 draft. Torvalds commented, "I think a number of outsiders... believed that I personally was just the odd man out because I've been so publicly not a huge fan of the GPLv3."<sup about="#mwt1286" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-seFzU_330-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"seFzU"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-seFzU-330"}}'><a href="#cite_note-seFzU-330" id="mwCdU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCdY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCdc">[</span>327<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCdg">]</span></span></a></sup> This group of high-profile kernel developers, including Torvalds, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Greg_Kroah-Hartman" title="Greg Kroah-Hartman" id="mwCdk">Greg Kroah-Hartman</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andrew_Morton_(computer_programmer)" title="Andrew Morton (computer programmer)" id="mwCdo">Andrew Morton</a>, commented on mass media about their objections to the GPLv3.<sup about="#mwt1287" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-kerneldevelopers2006_331-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"kerneldevelopers2006"}}'><a href="#cite_note-kerneldevelopers2006-331" id="mwCds"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCdw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCd0">[</span>328<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCd4">]</span></span></a></sup> They referred to clauses regarding <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Digital_rights_management" title="Digital rights management" id="mwCd8">DRM</a>/<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tivoization" title="Tivoization" id="mwCeA">tivoization</a>, patents, "additional restrictions" and warned a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balkanization" title="Balkanization" id="mwCeE">Balkanisation</a> of the "Open Source Universe" by the GPLv3.<sup about="#mwt1290" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-kerneldevelopers2006_331-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"kerneldevelopers2006"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-kerneldevelopers2006-331"}}'><a href="#cite_note-kerneldevelopers2006-331" id="mwCeI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCeM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCeQ">[</span>328<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCeU">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1293" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-GmA72_332-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"GmA72"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-GmA72-332"}}'><a href="#cite_note-GmA72-332" id="mwCeY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCec"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCeg">[</span>329<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCek">]</span></span></a></sup> Torvalds, who decided not to adopt the GPLv3 for the Linux kernel, reiterated his criticism even years later.<sup about="#mwt1296" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-4uolG_333-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"4uolG"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-4uolG-333"}}'><a href="#cite_note-4uolG-333" id="mwCeo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCes"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCew">[</span>330<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCe0">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="40" id="mwCe4" aria-labelledby="Loadable_kernel_modules"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Loadable_kernel_modules">Loadable kernel modules</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Linux_kernel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=40" title="Edit section: Loadable kernel modules"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwCe8">It is debated whether some <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Loadable_kernel_module" title="Loadable kernel module" id="mwCfA">loadable kernel modules</a> (LKMs) are to be considered <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Derivative_work" title="Derivative work" id="mwCfE">derivative works</a> under copyright law, and thereby whether or not they fall under the terms of the GPL.</p>

<p id="mwCfI">In accordance with the license rules, LKMs using only a public subset of the kernel interfaces<sup about="#mwt1297" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:6_238-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:6-238" id="mwCfM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCfQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCfU">[</span>235<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCfY">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1298" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_239-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-239" id="mwCfc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCfg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCfk">[</span>236<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCfo">]</span></span></a></sup> are non-derived works, thus Linux gives system administrators the mechanisms to load out-of-tree binary objects into the kernel address space.<sup about="#mwt1299" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Linux_Licensing_16-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Linux_Licensing"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Linux_Licensing-16" id="mwCfs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCfw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCf0">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCf4">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwCf8">There are some out-of-tree loadable modules that make legitimate use of the <i id="mwCgA">dma_buf</i> kernel feature.<sup about="#mwt1302" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-lkXX9_334-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"lkXX9"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-lkXX9-334"}}'><a href="#cite_note-lkXX9-334" id="mwCgE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCgI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCgM">[</span>331<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCgQ">]</span></span></a></sup> GPL compliant code can certainly use it. A different possible use case would be <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nvidia_Optimus" title="Nvidia Optimus" id="mwCgU">Nvidia Optimus</a> that pairs a fast GPU with an Intel integrated GPU, where the Nvidia GPU writes into the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Intel" title="Intel" id="mwCgY">Intel</a> framebuffer when it is active. But, Nvidia cannot use this infrastructure because it necessitates bypassing a rule that can only be used by LKMs that are also GPL.<sup about="#mwt1303" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:8_240-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":8"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:8-240" id="mwCgc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCgg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCgk">[</span>237<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCgo">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alan_Cox_(computer_programmer)" title="Alan Cox (computer programmer)" id="mwCgs">Alan Cox</a> replied on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Linux_kernel_mailing_list" title="Linux kernel mailing list" id="mwCgw">LKML</a>, rejecting a request from one of Nvidia's engineers to remove this technical enforcement from the API.<sup about="#mwt1306" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-1aNop_335-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"1aNop"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-1aNop-335"}}'><a href="#cite_note-1aNop-335" id="mwCg0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCg4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCg8">[</span>332<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwChA">]</span></span></a></sup> Torvalds clearly stated on the LKML that "[I] claim that binary-only kernel modules ARE derivative 'by default'".<sup about="#mwt1309" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-3OFDI_336-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"3OFDI"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-3OFDI-336"}}'><a href="#cite_note-3OFDI-336" id="mwChE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwChI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwChM">[</span>333<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwChQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwChU">On the other hand, Torvalds has also said that "[one] gray area in particular is something like a driver that was originally written for another operating system (i.e., clearly not a derived work of Linux in origin). THAT is a gray area, and _that_ is the area where I personally believe that some modules may be considered to not be derived works simply because they weren't designed for Linux and don't depend on any special Linux behaviour".<sup about="#mwt1312" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-H8s4M_337-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"H8s4M"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-H8s4M-337"}}'><a href="#cite_note-H8s4M-337" id="mwChY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwChc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwChg">[</span>334<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwChk">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proprietary_software" title="Proprietary software" id="mwCho">Proprietary</a> graphics drivers, in particular, are heavily discussed.</p>

<p id="mwChs">Whenever proprietary modules are loaded into Linux, the kernel marks itself as being "tainted",<sup about="#mwt1315" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BvXbe_338-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BvXbe"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-BvXbe-338"}}'><a href="#cite_note-BvXbe-338" id="mwChw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCh0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCh4">[</span>335<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCh8">]</span></span></a></sup> and therefore bug reports from tainted kernels will often be ignored by developers.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="41" id="mwCiA" aria-labelledby="Firmware_binary_blobs"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Firmware_binary_blobs">Firmware binary blobs</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Linux_kernel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=41" title="Edit section: Firmware binary blobs"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwCiE">The official kernel, that is, Torvalds's git branch at the kernel.org repository, contains <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Binary_blob" title="Binary blob" id="mwCiI">binary blobs</a> released under the terms of the GNU GPLv2 license.<sup about="#mwt1316" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-COPYING_File_11-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"COPYING_File"}}'><a href="#cite_note-COPYING_File-11" id="mwCiM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCiQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCiU">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCiY">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1317" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Linux_Licensing_16-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Linux_Licensing"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Linux_Licensing-16" id="mwCic"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCig"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCik">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCio">]</span></span></a></sup> Linux can also load binary blobs, proprietary firmware, drivers, or other executable modules from the filesystem, and link them into kernel space.<sup about="#mwt1320" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-rjGNf_339-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"rjGNf"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-rjGNf-339"}}'><a href="#cite_note-rjGNf-339" id="mwCis"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCiw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCi0">[</span>336<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCi4">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwCi8">When necessary (e.g., for accessing boot devices or for speed), firmware can be built-in to the kernel, meaning building the firmware into <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vmlinux" title="Vmlinux" id="mwCjA">vmlinux</a>; nonetheless, this is not always a viable option for technical or legal issues (e.g., it is not permitted to do this with firmware that is not GPL compatible, although this is quite common nonetheless).<sup about="#mwt1323" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-AEsBH_340-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"AEsBH"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-AEsBH-340"}}'><a href="#cite_note-AEsBH-340" id="mwCjE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCjI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCjM">[</span>337<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCjQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="42" id="mwCjU" aria-labelledby="Trademark"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Trademark">Trademark</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Linux_kernel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=42" title="Edit section: Trademark"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwCjY">Linux is a registered <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trademark" title="Trademark" id="mwCjc">trademark</a> of Linus Torvalds in the United States, the European Union, and some other countries.<sup about="#mwt1326" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Vaz3B_341-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Vaz3B"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Vaz3B-341"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Vaz3B-341" id="mwCjg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCjk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCjo">[</span>338<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCjs">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1329" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ID7gn_342-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ID7gn"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-ID7gn-342"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ID7gn-342" id="mwCjw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCj0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCj4">[</span>339<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCj8">]</span></span></a></sup> A legal battle over the trademark began in 1996, when William Della Croce, a lawyer who was never involved in the development of Linux, started requesting licensing fees for the use of the word <i id="mwCkA">Linux</i>. After it was proven that the word was in common use long before Della Croce's claimed first use, the trademark was awarded to Torvalds.<sup about="#mwt1332" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-dtKCp_343-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"dtKCp"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-dtKCp-343"}}'><a href="#cite_note-dtKCp-343" id="mwCkE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCkI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCkM">[</span>340<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCkQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1335" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ZWQ58_344-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ZWQ58"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-ZWQ58-344"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ZWQ58-344" id="mwCkU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCkY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCkc">[</span>341<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCkg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1338" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-05hM3_345-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"05hM3"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-05hM3-345"}}'><a href="#cite_note-05hM3-345" id="mwCkk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCko"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCks">[</span>342<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCkw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="43" id="mwCk0" aria-labelledby="Removal_of_Russian_maintainers"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Removal_of_Russian_maintainers">Removal of Russian maintainers</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Linux_kernel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=43" title="Edit section: Removal of Russian maintainers"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwCk4">In October 2024, during the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_invasion_of_Ukraine" title="Russian invasion of Ukraine" class="mw-redirect" id="mwCk8">Russian invasion of Ukraine</a>, kernel developer <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Greg_Kroah-Hartman" title="Greg Kroah-Hartman" id="mwClA">Greg Kroah-Hartman</a> removed some kernel developers whose email addresses suggested a connection to Russia from their roles as maintainers.<sup about="#mwt1341" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-346" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-346"}}'><a href="#cite_note-346" id="mwClE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwClI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwClM">[</span>343<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwClQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt1344" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-347" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-347"}}'><a href="#cite_note-347" id="mwClU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwClY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwClc">[</span>344<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwClg">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Linus_Torvalds" title="Linus Torvalds" id="mwClk">Linus Torvalds</a> responded that he did not support Russian aggression and would not revert the patch, insinuating that opponents of the patch were <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_web_brigades" title="Russian web brigades" id="mwClo">Russian trolls</a>.<sup about="#mwt1347" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-348" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-348"}}'><a href="#cite_note-348" id="mwCls"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwClw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCl0">[</span>345<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCl4">]</span></span></a></sup> James Bottomley, a kernel developer, issued an apology for the handling of the situation and clarified that the action was a consequence of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_sanctions_during_the_Russian_invasion_of_Ukraine" title="International sanctions during the Russian invasion of Ukraine" id="mwCl8">U.S. sanctions against Russia</a>.<sup about="#mwt1350" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-349" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-349"}}'><a href="#cite_note-349" id="mwCmA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCmE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCmI">[</span>346<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCmM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="44" id="mwCmQ" aria-labelledby="See_also"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="See_also">See also</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Linux_kernel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=44" title="Edit section: See also"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<li about="#cite_note-v5UoJ-328" id="cite_note-v5UoJ-328" data-mw-footnote-number="325"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwGYI"><a href="#cite_ref-v5UoJ_328-0" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwGYM"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwGYQ">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-v5UoJ-328" class="mw-refereinister <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neville_Chamberlain" title="Neville Chamberlain" id="mwAjw">Neville Chamberlain</a> and conceded this territory to Germany in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Munich_Agreement" title="Munich Agreement" id="mwAj0">Munich Agreement</a>, which was made against the wishes of the Czechoslovak government, in exchange for a promise of no further territorial demands.<sup about="#mwt231" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001121–122_57-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEKershaw2001121–122"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001121–122-57"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Kershaw"},"2":{"wt":"2001"},"pp":{"wt":"121–122"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001121–122-57" id="mwAj4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAj8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAkA">[</span>55<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAkE">]</span></span></a></sup> Soon afterwards, Germany and Italy forced Czechoslovakia to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/First_Vienna_Award" title="First Vienna Award" id="mwAkI">cede additional territory</a> to Hungary, and Poland annexed the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trans-Olza" title="Trans-Olza" id="mwAkM">Trans-Olza</a> region of Czechoslovakia.<sup about="#mwt234" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001157_58-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEKershaw2001157"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001157-58"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Kershaw"},"2":{"wt":"2001"},"p":{"wt":"157"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001157-58" id="mwAkQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAkU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAkY">[</span>56<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAkc">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAkg">Although all of Germany's stated demands had been satisfied by the agreement, privately Hitler was furious that British interference had prevented him from seizing all of Czechoslovakia in one operation. In subsequent speeches Hitler attacked British and Jewish "war-mongers" and in January 1939 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Plan_Z" title="Plan Z" id="mwAkk">secretly ordered a major build-up of the German navy</a> to challenge British naval supremacy. In March 1939, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Occupation_of_Czechoslovakia_(1938–1945)" title="Occupation of Czechoslovakia (1938–1945)" id="mwAko">Germany invaded the remainder of Czechoslovakia</a> and subsequently split it into the German <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Protectorate_of_Bohemia_and_Moravia" title="Protectorate of Bohemia and Moravia" id="mwAks">Protectorate of Bohemia and Moravia</a> and a pro-German <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Client_state" title="Client state" id="mwAkw">client state</a>, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Slovak_Republic_(1939–1945)" title="Slovak Republic (1939–1945)" id="mwAk0">Slovak Republic</a>.<sup about="#mwt237" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEDavies2006pp._143–144_(2008_ed.)_59-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEDavies2006pp. 143–144 (2008 ed.)"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEDavies2006pp._143–144_(2008_ed.)-59"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Davies"},"2":{"wt":"2006"},"loc":{"wt":"pp. 143–144 (2008 ed.)"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEDavies2006pp._143–144_(2008_ed.)-59" id="mwAk4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAk8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlA">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlE">]</span></span></a></sup> Hitler also delivered an <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1939_German_ultimatum_to_Lithuania" title="1939 German ultimatum to Lithuania" id="mwAlI">ultimatum to Lithuania</a> on 20 March 1939, forcing the concession of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Klaipėda_Region" title="Klaipėda Region" id="mwAlM">Klaipėda Region</a>, formerly the German <i id="mwAlQ">Memelland</i>.<sup about="#mwt240" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEShirer1990461–462_60-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEShirer1990461–462"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEShirer1990461–462-60"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Shirer"},"2":{"wt":"1990"},"pp":{"wt":"461–462"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEShirer1990461–462-60" id="mwAlU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAlY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlc">[</span>58<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAlk">Following further demands from Hitler regarding the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_City_of_Danzig" title="Free City of Danzig" id="mwAlo">Free City of Danzig</a> and the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polish_corridor" title="Polish corridor" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAls">Polish corridor</a>, the United Kingdom and France <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anglo-Polish_alliance#British_assurance_to_Poland" title="Anglo-Polish alliance" id="mwAlw">guaranteed their support for Polish independence</a>. When <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_invasion_of_Albania" title="Italian invasion of Albania" id="mwAl0">Italy conquered Albania</a> in April 1939, the same guarantee was extended to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kingdom_of_Romania" title="Kingdom of Romania" id="mwAl4">Kingdoms of Romania</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kingdom_of_Greece" title="Kingdom of Greece" id="mwAl8">Greece</a>.<sup about="#mwt243" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTELoweMarzari2002330_61-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTELoweMarzari2002330"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTELoweMarzari2002330-61"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Lowe"},"2":{"wt":"Marzari"},"3":{"wt":"2002"},"p":{"wt":"330"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTELoweMarzari2002330-61" id="mwAmA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAmE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmI">[</span>59<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmM">]</span></span></a></sup> Shortly after the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Franco-Polish_alliance" title="Franco-Polish alliance" id="mwAmQ">Franco</a>-<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anglo-Polish_alliance" title="Anglo-Polish alliance" id="mwAmU">British</a> pledge to Poland, Germany and Italy formalised their own alliance with the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pact_of_Steel" title="Pact of Steel" id="mwAmY">Pact of Steel</a>.<sup about="#mwt246" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEDearFoot2001234_62-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEDearFoot2001234"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEDearFoot2001234-62"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Dear"},"2":{"wt":"Foot"},"3":{"wt":"2001"},"p":{"wt":"234"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEDearFoot2001234-62" id="mwAmc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAmg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmk">[</span>60<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmo">]</span></span></a></sup> Hitler accused the United Kingdom and Poland of trying to encircle Germany and renounced the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anglo-German_Naval_Agreement" title="Anglo-German Naval Agreement" id="mwAms">Anglo-German Naval Agreement</a> and the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/German–Polish_declaration_of_non-aggression" title="German–Polish declaration of non-aggression" id="mwAmw">German–Polish declaration of non-aggression</a>.<sup about="#mwt249" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEShirer1990471_63-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEShirer1990471"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEShirer1990471-63"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Shirer"},"2":{"wt":"1990"},"p":{"wt":"471"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEShirer1990471-63" id="mwAm0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAm4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAm8">[</span>61<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-right" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwAnE"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Bundesarchiv_Bild_183-H27337,_Moskau,_Stalin_und_Ribbentrop_im_Kreml.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwAnI"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Bundesarchiv_Bild_183-H27337,_Moskau,_Stalin_und_Ribbentrop_im_Kreml.jpg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/38/Bundesarchiv_Bild_183-H27337%2C_Moskau%2C_Stalin_und_Ribbentrop_im_Kreml.jpg/250px-Bundesarchiv_Bild_183-H27337%2C_Moskau%2C_Stalin_und_Ribbentrop_im_Kreml.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/38/Bundesarchiv_Bild_183-H27337%2C_Moskau%2C_Stalin_und_Ribbentrop_im_Kreml.jpg/500px-Bundesarchiv_Bild_183-H27337%2C_Moskau%2C_Stalin_und_Ribbentrop_im_Kreml.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="1185" data-file-height="1562" data-file-type="bitmap" height="250" width="190" class="mw-file-element mw-file-upright" style="--mw-file-upright: 0.75" id="mwAnM"/></a><figcaption id="mwAnQ">German Foreign Minister <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joachim_von_Ribbentrop" title="Joachim von Ribbentrop" id="mwAnU">Joachim von Ribbentrop</a> (right) and the Soviet leader <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joseph_Stalin" title="Joseph Stalin" id="mwAnY">Joseph Stalin</a> after signing the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Molotov–Ribbentrop_Pact" title="Molotov–Ribbentrop Pact" id="mwAnc">Molotov–Ribbentrop Pact</a>, 23 August 1939</figcaption></figure>

<p id="mwAng">The situation became a crisis in late August as Germany concentrated its forces near the Polish border. On 23 August, the Soviet Union signed <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Molotov–Ribbentrop_Pact" title="Molotov–Ribbentrop Pact" id="mwAnk">a non-aggression pact</a> with Germany, after tripartite negotiations for a military alliance between France, the United Kingdom, and Soviet Union had stalled.<sup about="#mwt252" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEShore2003108_64-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEShore2003108"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEShore2003108-64"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Shore"},"2":{"wt":"2003"},"p":{"wt":"108"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEShore2003108-64" id="mwAno"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAns"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnw">[</span>62<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAn0">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt263" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-65" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-65"}}'><a href="#cite_note-65" id="mwAn4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAn8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoA">[</span>63<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoE">]</span></span></a></sup> This pact had a secret protocol that defined German and Soviet spheres of influence: Lithuania for Germany; Finland, Estonia, Latvia, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bessarabia" title="Bessarabia" id="mwAoI">Bessarabia</a> for the Soviet Union; and Poland to be partitioned between the two powers.<sup about="#mwt255" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEDearFoot2001608_66-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEDearFoot2001608"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEDearFoot2001608-66"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Dear"},"2":{"wt":"Foot"},"3":{"wt":"2001"},"p":{"wt":"608"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEDearFoot2001608-66" id="mwAoM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAoQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoU">[</span>64<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoY">]</span></span></a></sup> The pact ensured that Germany would not face a war with the Soviet Union when it invaded Poland. Immediately afterwards, Hitler ordered the attack to proceed on 26 August, but upon hearing that the United Kingdom had concluded a formal mutual assistance pact with Poland and that Italy would maintain neutrality, he decided to delay it.<sup about="#mwt258" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEWeinberg199537–42_67-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEWeinberg199537–42"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeinberg199537–42-67"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Weinberg"},"2":{"wt":"1995"},"pp":{"wt":"37–42"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeinberg199537–42-67" id="mwAoc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAog"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAok">[</span>65<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoo">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAos">Germany offered Britain an alliance if Britain helped Germany gain Danzig, the Polish corridor and its former colonies, but Britain replied that it would honour its guarantee to Poland.<sup about="#mwt264" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001216_68-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEKershaw2001216"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001216-68"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Kershaw"},"2":{"wt":"2001"},"p":{"wt":"216"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001216-68" id="mwAow"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAo0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAo4">[</span>66<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAo8">]</span></span></a></sup> On 29 August, Hitler demanded that a Polish <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Plenipotentiary" title="Plenipotentiary" id="mwApA">plenipotentiary</a> travel to Berlin by the following day to negotiate a solution to the crisis, but Britain rejected the timeline as unreasonable. On the night of 30–31 August, the British ambassador <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nevile_Henderson" title="Nevile Henderson" id="mwApE">Nevile Henderson</a> met German Foreign Minister <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joachim_von_Ribbentrop" title="Joachim von Ribbentrop" id="mwApI">Joachim von Ribbentrop</a>. Ribbentrop handed him Hitler's demands regarding Poland then announced that the deadline for Poland's acceptance had already passed.<sup about="#mwt267" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001218–219_69-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEKershaw2001218–219"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001218–219-69"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Kershaw"},"2":{"wt":"2001"},"pp":{"wt":"218–219"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEKershaw2001218–219-69" id="mwApM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwApQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwApU">[</span>67<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwApY">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="13" id="mwApc" aria-labelledby="Course_of_the_war"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Course_of_the_war">Course of the war</h2></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt270" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwApg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"For timeline","href":"./Template:For_timeline"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"List of timelines of World War II"}},"i":0}}]}'><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt271" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'/></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt270" id="mwApk">For a chronological guide, see <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_timelines_of_World_War_II" title="List of timelines of World War II">List of timelines of World War II</a>.</div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt272" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwApo" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"See also","href":"./Template:See_also"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Diplomatic history of World War II"},"2":{"wt":"World War II by country"}},"i":0}}]}'><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt273" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'/></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt272" id="mwAps">See also: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diplomatic_history_of_World_War_II" title="Diplomatic history of World War II">Diplomatic history of World War II</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_War_II_by_country" title="World War II by country">World War II by country</a></div>

<section data-mw-section-id="14" id="mwApw" aria-labelledby="War_breaks_out_in_Europe_(1939–1940)"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="War_breaks_out_in_Europe_(1939–1940)"><span id="War_breaks_out_in_Europe_.281939.E2.80.931940.29" typeof="mw:FallbackId"></span>War breaks out in Europe (1939–1940)</h3></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt274" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAp0" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Main","href":"./Template:Main"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"European theatre of World War II"}},"i":0}}]}'><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt275" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'/></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt274" id="mwAp4">Main article: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_theatre_of_World_War_II" title="European theatre of World War II">European theatre of World War II</a></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwAp8"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Germans_at_Polish_Border_(1939-09-01).jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwAqA"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Germans_at_Polish_Border_(1939-09-01).jpg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a7/Danzig_Police_at_Polish_Border_%281939-09-01%29.jpg/250px-Danzig_Police_at_Polish_Border_%281939-09-01%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a7/Danzig_Police_at_Polish_Border_%281939-09-01%29.jpg/500px-Danzig_Police_at_Polish_Border_%281939-09-01%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="1500" data-file-height="1033" data-file-type="bitmap" height="172" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwAqE"/></a><figcaption id="mwAqI">A <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Propaganda_in_Nazi_Germany" title="Propaganda in Nazi Germany" id="mwAqM">German propaganda</a> photograph reenacting the removal of the Polish border crossing in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sopot" title="Sopot" id="mwAqQ">Sopot</a><sup about="#mwt278" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-70" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-70"}}'><a href="#cite_note-70" id="mwAqU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAqY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqc">[</span>68<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqg">]</span></span></a></sup></figcaption></figure>

<p id="mwAqk">On 1 September 1939, Germany <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Invaded_Poland" title="Invaded Poland" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAqo">invaded Poland</a> after <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operation_Himmler" title="Operation Himmler" id="mwAqs">having staged</a> several <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gleiwitz_incident" title="Gleiwitz incident" id="mwAqw">false flag border incidents</a> as a pretext to initiate the invasion.<sup about="#mwt279" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEEvans20081–2_71-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEEvans20081–2"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEEvans20081–2-71"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Evans"},"2":{"wt":"2008"},"pp":{"wt":"1–2"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEEvans20081–2-71" id="mwAq0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAq4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAq8">[</span>69<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArA">]</span></span></a></sup> The first German attack of the war came <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_of_Westerplatte" title="Battle of Westerplatte" id="mwArE">against the Polish defences at Westerplatte</a>.<sup about="#mwt287" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Zabecki2015_72-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Zabecki2015"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Zabecki2015-72"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Zabecki2015-72" id="mwArI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwArM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArQ">[</span>70<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArU">]</span></span></a></sup> The United Kingdom responded with an <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ultimatum" title="Ultimatum" id="mwArY">ultimatum</a> for Germany to cease military operations, and on 3 September, after the ultimatum was ignored, Britain and France declared war on Germany.<sup about="#mwt282" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-73" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"lower-alpha"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-73"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"efn","href":"./Template:Efn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"[[United Kingdom declaration of war on Germany (1939)|The UK declared war on Germany]] at 11&amp;nbsp;am. [[French declaration of war on Germany (1939)|France followed 6 hours later]] at 5&amp;nbsp;pm."}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-73" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwArc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwArg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArk">[</span>c<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAro">]</span></span></a></sup> During the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Phoney_War" title="Phoney War" id="mwArs">Phoney War</a> period, the alliance provided no direct military support to Poland, outside of a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saar_Offensive" title="Saar Offensive" id="mwArw">cautious French probe into the Saarland</a>.<sup about="#mwt292" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Keegan_1997_35_74-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Keegan 1997 35"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Keegan_1997_35-74"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Keegan_1997_35-74" id="mwAr0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAr4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAr8">[</span>71<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsA">]</span></span></a></sup> The Western Allies also began a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blockade_of_Germany_(1939–1945)" title="Blockade of Germany (1939–1945)" id="mwAsE">naval blockade of Germany</a>, which aimed to damage the country's economy and war effort.<sup about="#mwt296" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-75" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-75"}}'><a href="#cite_note-75" id="mwAsI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAsM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsQ">[</span>72<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsU">]</span></span></a></sup> Germany responded by ordering <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Submarine_warfare#World_War_II" title="Submarine warfare" id="mwAsY">U-boat warfare</a> against Allied merchant and warships, which would later escalate into the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_of_the_Atlantic" title="Battle of the Atlantic" id="mwAsc">Battle of the Atlantic</a>.<sup about="#mwt299" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-76" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-76"}}'><a href="#cite_note-76" id="mwAsg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAsk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAso">[</span>73<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAss">]</span></span></a></sup>
On 8 September, German troops reached the suburbs of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Warsaw" title="Warsaw" id="mwAsw">Warsaw</a>. The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_of_the_Bzura" title="Battle of the Bzura" id="mwAs0">Polish counter-offensive</a> to the west halted the German advance for several days, but it was outflanked and encircled by the <i id="mwAs4"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wehrmacht" title="Wehrmacht" id="mwAs8">Wehrmacht</a></i>. Remnants of the Polish army broke through to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Siege_of_Warsaw_(1939)" title="Siege of Warsaw (1939)" id="mwAtA">besieged Warsaw</a>. On 17 September 1939, two days after signing a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battles_of_Khalkhin_Gol" title="Battles of Khalkhin Gol" id="mwAtE">cease-fire with Japan</a>, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soviet_invasion_of_Poland" title="Soviet invasion of Poland" id="mwAtI">Soviet Union invaded Poland</a><sup about="#mwt300" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEZaloga200280,_83_77-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEZaloga200280, 83"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEZaloga200280,_83-77"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Zaloga"},"2":{"wt":"2002"},"pp":{"wt":"80, 83"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEZaloga200280,_83-77" id="mwAtM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAtQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAtU">[</span>74<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAtY">]</span></span></a></sup> under the supposed pretext that the Polish state had ceased to exist.<sup about="#mwt311" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-78" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-78"}}'><a href="#cite_note-78" id="mwAtc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAtg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAtk">[</span>75<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAto">]</span></span></a></sup> On 27 September, the Warsaw garrison surrendered to the Germans, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Independent_Operational_Group_Polesie" title="Independent Operational Group Polesie" id="mwAts">the last large operational unit of the Polish Army</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_of_Kock_(1939)" title="Battle of Kock (1939)" id="mwAtw">surrendered on 6<span class="nowrap" about="#mwt312" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAt0" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"nbsp","href":"./Template:Nbsp"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}'><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span>October</a>. Despite the military defeat, Poland never surrendered; instead, it formed the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polish_government-in-exile" title="Polish government-in-exile" id="mwAt4">Polish government-in-exile</a> and a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polish_Underground_State" title="Polish Underground State" id="mwAt8">clandestine state apparatus remained</a> in occupied Poland.<sup about="#mwt303" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEHempel200524_79-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEHempel200524"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEHempel200524-79"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Hempel"},"2":{"wt":"2005"},"p":{"wt":"24"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEHempel200524-79" id="mwAuA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAuE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuI">[</span>76<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuM">]</span></span></a></sup> A significant part of Polish military personnel <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romanian_Bridgehead" title="Romanian Bridgehead" id="mwAuQ">evacuated to Romania</a> and Latvia; many of them later <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Military_history_of_Poland_during_World_War_II" title="Military history of Poland during World War II" id="mwAuU">fought against the Axis</a> in other theatres of the war.<sup about="#mwt306" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEZaloga200288–89_80-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEZaloga200288–89"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEZaloga200288–89-80"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Zaloga"},"2":{"wt":"2002"},"pp":{"wt":"88–89"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEZaloga200288–89-80" id="mwAuY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAuc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAug">[</span>77<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuk">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAuo">Germany <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polish_areas_annexed_by_Nazi_Germany" title="Polish areas annexed by Nazi Germany" id="mwAus">annexed western Poland</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/General_Government" title="General Government" id="mwAuw">occupied central Poland</a>; the Soviet Union <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Territories_of_Poland_annexed_by_the_Soviet_Union" title="Territories of Poland annexed by the Soviet Union" id="mwAu0">annexed eastern Poland</a>. Small shares of Polish territory were transferred to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Territorial_evolution_of_Poland#World_War_II" title="Territorial evolution of Poland" id="mwAu4">Lithuania</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Slovak_invasion_of_Poland" title="Slovak invasion of Poland" id="mwAu8">Slovakia</a>. On 6 October, Hitler made a public peace overture to the United Kingdom and France but said that the future of Poland was to be determined exclusively by Germany and the Soviet Union. The proposal was rejected<sup about="#mwt321" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ibiblio1939_81-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ibiblio1939"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-ibiblio1939-81"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ibiblio1939-81" id="mwAvA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAvE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvI">[</span>78<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvM">]</span></span></a></sup> and Hitler ordered an immediate offensive against France,<sup about="#mwt322" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-82" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-82"}}'><a href="#cite_note-82" id="mwAvQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAvU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvY">[</span>79<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvc">]</span></span></a></sup> which was postponed until the spring of 1940 due to bad weather.<sup about="#mwt313" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTELiddell_Hart197739–40_83-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTELiddell Hart197739–40"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTELiddell_Hart197739–40-83"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Liddell Hart"},"2":{"wt":"1977"},"pp":{"wt":"39–40"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTELiddell_Hart197739–40-83" id="mwAvg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAvk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvo">[</span>80<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvs">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt316" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEBullock1990pp._563–564,_566,_568–569,_574–575_(1983_ed.)_84-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEBullock1990pp. 563–564, 566, 568–569, 574–575 (1983 ed.)"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEBullock1990pp._563–564,_566,_568–569,_574–575_(1983_ed.)-84"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Bullock"},"2":{"wt":"1990"},"loc":{"wt":"pp. 563–564, 566, 568–569, 574–575 (1983 ed.)"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEBullock1990pp._563–564,_566,_568–569,_574–575_(1983_ed.)-84" id="mwAvw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAv0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAv4">[</span>81<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAv8">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt325" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-85" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-85"}}'><a href="#cite_note-85" id="mwAwA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAwE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAwI">[</span>82<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAwM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwAwQ"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Karelian_Isthmus_13_March_1940.png" class="mw-file-description" id="mwAwU"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Karelian_Isthmus_13_March_1940.png" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0b/Karelian_Isthmus_13_March_1940.png/250px-Karelian_Isthmus_13_March_1940.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0b/Karelian_Isthmus_13_March_1940.png/500px-Karelian_Isthmus_13_March_1940.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="546" data-file-height="492" data-file-type="bitmap" height="225" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwAwY"/></a><figcaption id="mwAwc"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mannerheim_Line" title="Mannerheim Line" id="mwAwg">Mannerheim Line</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karelian_Isthmus" title="Karelian Isthmus" id="mwAwk">Karelian Isthmus</a> on the last day of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Winter_War" title="Winter War" id="mwAwo">Winter War</a>, 13 March 1940</figcaption></figure>

<p id="mwAws">After the outbreak of war in Poland, Stalin threatened Estonia, Latvia, and Lithuania with military invasion, forcing the three <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baltic_countries" title="Baltic countries" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAww">Baltic countries</a> to sign <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Background_of_the_occupation_of_the_Baltic_states#Soviet_ultimatums_and_occupation" title="Background of the occupation of the Baltic states" id="mwAw0">pacts</a> allowing the creation of Soviet military bases in these countries; in October 1939, significant Soviet military contingents were moved there.<sup about="#mwt326" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTESmithPabriksPursLane200224_86-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTESmithPabriksPursLane200224"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTESmithPabriksPursLane200224-86"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Smith"},"2":{"wt":"Pabriks"},"3":{"wt":"Purs"},"4":{"wt":"Lane"},"5":{"wt":"2002"},"p":{"wt":"24"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTESmithPabriksPursLane200224-86" id="mwAw4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAw8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAxA">[</span>83<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAxE">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt329" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEBilinsky19999_87-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEBilinsky19999"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEBilinsky19999-87"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Bilinsky"},"2":{"wt":"1999"},"p":{"wt":"9"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEBilinsky19999-87" id="mwAxI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAxM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAxQ">[</span>84<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAxU">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt332" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEMurrayMillett200155–56_88-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEMurrayMillett200155–56"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEMurrayMillett200155–56-88"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Murray"},"2":{"wt":"Millett"},"3":{"wt":"2001"},"pp":{"wt":"55–56"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEMurrayMillett200155–56-88" id="mwAxY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAxc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAxg">[</span>85<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAxk">]</span></span></a></sup> Finland refused to sign a similar pact and rejected ceding part of its territory to the Soviet Union. <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Winter_War#Soviet_invasion" title="Winter War" id="mwAxo">The Soviet Union invaded Finland</a> in November 1939,<sup about="#mwt335" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTESpring1986207–226_89-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTESpring1986207–226"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTESpring1986207–226-89"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Spring"},"2":{"wt":"1986"},"pp":{"wt":"207–226"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTESpring1986207–226-89" id="mwAxs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAxw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAx0">[</span>86<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAx4">]</span></span></a></sup> and was subsequently expelled from the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/League_of_Nations" title="League of Nations" id="mwAx8">League of Nations</a> for this crime of aggression.<sup about="#mwt343" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-90" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-90"}}'><a href="#cite_note-90" id="mwAyA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAyE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAyI">[</span>87<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAyM">]</span></span></a></sup> Despite overwhelming numerical superiority, Soviet military success during the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Winter_War" title="Winter War" id="mwAyQ">Winter War</a> was modest, and the Finno–Soviet war ended in March 1940 with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moscow_Peace_Treaty" title="Moscow Peace Treaty" id="mwAyU">some Finnish concessions of territory</a>.<sup about="#mwt338" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEHanhimäki199712_91-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEHanhimäki199712"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEHanhimäki199712-91"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Hanhimäki"},"2":{"wt":"1997"},"p":{"wt":"12"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEHanhimäki199712-91" id="mwAyY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAyc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAyg">[</span>88<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAyk">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAyo">In June 1940, the Soviet Union <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soviet_occupation_of_the_Baltic_states_(1940)" title="Soviet occupation of the Baltic states (1940)" id="mwAys">occupied</a> the entire territories of Estonia, Latvia, and Lithuania,<sup about="#mwt344" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEBilinsky19999_87-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEBilinsky19999"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTEBilinsky19999-87"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sfn","href":"./Template:Sfn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Bilinsky"},"2":{"wt":"1999"},"p":{"wt":"9"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEBilinsky19999-87" id="mwAyw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAy0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAy4">[</span>84<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAy8">]</span></span></a></sup> as well as the Romanian regions of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soviet_occupation_of_Bessarabia_and_Northern_Bukovina" title="Soviet occupation of Bessarabia and Northern Bukovina" id="mwAzA">Bessarabia, Northern Bukovina, and the Hertsa region</a>. In August 1940, Hitler imposed the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Second_Vienna_Award" title="Second Vienna Award" id="mwAzE">Second Vienna Award</a> on Romania which led to the transfer of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northern_Transylvania" title="Northern Transylvania" id="mwAzI">Northern Transylvania</a> to Hungary.<sup about="#mwt347" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEDearFoot2001745,_975_92-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"","name":"FOOTNOTEDearFoot2001745,
